# Skills · G · part 50

874 entries, gibbs-reflective-cycle to gis-geoprocessing-specialist. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **gibbs-reflective-cycle**: Use this skill when the user wants guided Gibbs' Reflective Cycle applied to real work. It teaches the source-grounded method, halts for observable user work, evaluates each gate, and produces a structured handoff. ([cerredz/Vidbyte-Skills](https://github.com/cerredz/Vidbyte-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gibbs-reflective-cycle))
- **gibbs-sampler-clustering-of-sequencing-reads**: Use when error-prone shotgun or amplicon NGS reads (e.g. 454 Titanium pyrosequencing of HIV-1 pol) aligned to a reference must be partitioned into local-haplotype clusters to correct sequencing errors before quantifying genetic diversity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/gibbs-sampler-clustering-of-sequencing-reads))
- **gibbs-sampler-convergence-assessment**: Use when runModels has built several LDA models (e.g. 2–40 topics) on single-cell ATAC-seq data and you must verify the log-likelihood stabilized after burnin and pick the model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/gibbs-sampler-convergence-assessment))
- **gibbs-sampler-convergence-tuning**: Use when running diri_sampler-based error correction on overlapping windows of a read alignment (BAM/CRAM or MSA) and you must set sampler iterations and the Dirichlet hyperparameter alpha to achieve proper mixing for haplotype reconstruction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/gibbs-sampler-convergence-tuning))
- **gibbs-sampler-implementation**: Use when a targeted LC/MS or GC/MS metabolomics matrix contains left-censored missing values attributed to detection limits and requires MNAR-aware imputation before downstream univariate or multivariate analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gibbs-sampler-implementation))
- **gibbs-sampler-implementation**: Use when clustering droplet-based UMI count matrices from multiple individuals with BAMM-SC and you must run, tune, or validate the MCMC/Gibbs sampler (burn-in, iteration budget, log-likelihood tolerance) behind the hierarchical Dirichlet multinomial mixture. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gibbs-sampler-implementation))
- **gibbs-sampler-implementation-and-convergence**: Use when your metabolomics dataset contains missing values below a known detection limit (left-censored MNAR data), and you need to recover these values while respecting the truncation constraint. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/gibbs-sampler-implementation-and-convergence) · 3 copies)
- **gibbs-sampler-imputation-parameterization**: Use when a targeted metabolomics intensity matrix (e.g. FFA/BA LC-MS data) contains left-censored missing values attributed to LOD/LOQ (MNAR) and an iterative Gibbs sampler must be configured — iteration counts, initialization method, and upper/lower truncation bounds — before running GS_impute. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gibbs-sampler-imputation-parameterization))
- **gibbs-sampler-two-genome-mixture-modeling**: Use when single-cell allele-level UMI counts at filtered SNVs are available and the goal is to detect a minor genotype, quantify mixing fraction, or decide K=1 vs K=2 in in silico or wet-lab chimerism mixtures. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gibbs-sampler-two-genome-mixture-modeling))
- **gibbs-sampling-bayesian-inference**: Use when SIRIUS top-ranked molecular formulas are untrusted — e.g. metabolites above 500 Da in a compound network from untargeted LC-MS/MS — and top-50 candidate lists plus adduct assignments are available for network-based reranking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gibbs-sampling-bayesian-inference))
- **gibbs-sampling-mcmc-implementation**: Use when scRNA-seq count matrices contain pervasive dropouts (40%-90% missing values) and cell-to-cell relationships must be recovered via Bayesian matrix factorization with MCMC, optionally leveraging cell labels or bulk side information. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gibbs-sampling-mcmc-implementation))
- **gibbs-sampling-synthetic-count-generation**: Use when a trained scDBM (BoltzmannMachines.jl-based model on scRNA-seq count data) must be turned into synthetic observations of a target cell count, and when comparing sampling strategies such as Gibbs sampling against scVI prior/posterior sampling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gibbs-sampling-synthetic-count-generation))
- **gibbs-state-analysis**: Analysis of high-temperature Gibbs states with rapid mixing and external field effects.
